The SFT excludes selected PS-0417 row129 before parsing its payload, and reads no p951 stage vector, K04 mask, acceptance digest, or selected ACK bytes. The omitted predecessor MSB is not observed for any of the 17, even after all relations are included. A subsequent dependence audit qualifies the 10 graph-implied relations: only 3/10 survive deletion of any single fullword anchor, and 0/10 survive removing all anchors touching any of their three packet rows. Only 3/10 survive excluding exact recurrence-input-word overlaps. The initial word-position test was too narrow because a fullword anchor at source lane j consumes both lane j−1 and lane j; its lane-j relation tuple therefore still reuses the firstword relation's lane-0 source word. All 42 relations are monotone-feasible. The equality graph has 83 components; row129 remains isolated and its source-only start interval is 917–971. Two deterministic 200,000-trial whole-packet shuffle nulls (CLASS×DEST and CLASS×DEST×STATE×ATTEMPT) each have maximum 37 total relations; zero reach 42.
